              CLEAR : lv_level.
              lv_level = '3'.

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& Form FCAT
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& text
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      --> P_
*&      --> P_
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM fcat  USING    VALUE(p_1)
                    VALUE(p_2)
                    VALUE(p_3).

  IF rad6 = abap_true. " 01.07.2023 | Aishwarya | SAPAMS-303 | MSDK909846
    wa_fcat-lowercase = 'X'.
    wa_fcat-tabname = 'IT_FINAL'.
    ls_fcat-lowercase = 'X'.
    ls_fcat-tabname = 'IT_FINAL'.

    IF p_1 = 'LIFNR' OR
       p_1 = 'NAME1' .
      ls_fcat-key = abap_true.
      wa_fcat-key = abap_true.
    ENDIF.

    IF p_1 CS 'CMT'.
      ls_fcat-outputlen = 30.
      wa_fcat-outputlen = 30.
    ENDIF.


  ELSEIF rad7 = abap_true.
    wa_fcat-lowercase = 'X'.
    wa_fcat-tabname = 'IT_FINAL_NEW'.
    ls_fcat-lowercase = 'X'.
    ls_fcat-tabname = 'IT_FINAL_NEW'.

    IF p_1 = 'LIFNR' OR
       p_1 = 'NAME1' .
      ls_fcat-key = abap_true.
      wa_fcat-key = abap_true.
    ENDIF.

    IF p_1 CS 'CMT'.
      ls_fcat-outputlen = 30.
      wa_fcat-outputlen = 30.
    ENDIF.
  ELSE.
    wa_fcat-lowercase = 'X'.
    wa_fcat-tabname = 'IT_FINAL_NEW'.
    ls_fcat-lowercase = 'X'.
    ls_fcat-tabname = 'IT_FINAL_NEW'.

    IF p_1 = 'INCLUDE-LIFNR' OR
       p_1 = 'INCLUDE-NAME1'.
      ls_fcat-key = abap_true.
      wa_fcat-key = abap_true.
    ENDIF.

    IF p_1 CS 'INCLUDE-CMT'.
      ls_fcat-outputlen = 30.
      wa_fcat-outputlen = 30.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.
* end 01.07.2023 | Aishwarya | SAPAMS-303 | MSDK909846

  IF rad7 = abap_true.
    wa_fcat-fieldname = p_1.
    wa_fcat-edit = p_2.
    wa_fcat-seltext_l = p_3.
    IF wa_fcat-fieldname = 'CHK'.
      wa_fcat-checkbox = abap_true.
    ELSE.
      wa_fcat-checkbox = abap_false.
    ENDIF.
    APPEND wa_fcat TO it_fcat.
    CLEAR wa_fcat.
    IF p_arch IS INITIAL AND ( p_1 = 'SEQ' OR p_1 = 'AIND' OR p_1 = 'ADT' ).
      DELETE it_fcat WHERE fieldname = p_1.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

  IF rad6 = abap_false.

    ls_fcat-fieldname = p_1.
    ls_fcat-edit      = p_2.
    ls_fcat-coltext   = p_3.

    IF ls_fcat-fieldname EQ 'INCLUDE-DESC1'.
      ls_fcat-ref_table  = 'ZST_EKNAM'.
      ls_fcat-ref_field  = 'EKNAM'.
      ls_fcat-f4availabl = 'X'.
    ENDIF.

    IF ls_fcat-fieldname EQ 'INCLUDE-ZLOM'.
*      ls_fcat-reptext  = 'Material Safety Data Sheet (MSDS) is provided & relevant'.

    ENDIF.


    IF ls_fcat-fieldname EQ 'INCLUDE-CMT1' OR ls_fcat-fieldname EQ 'INCLUDE-CMT2'
      OR ls_fcat-fieldname EQ 'INCLUDE-CMT3' OR ls_fcat-fieldname EQ 'INCLUDE-CMT4'
      OR ls_fcat-fieldname EQ 'INCLUDE-CMT5' .
      ls_fcat-outputlen = '125'.
    ENDIF.
    IF ls_fcat-fieldname EQ 'INCLUDE-LIFNR' OR ls_fcat-fieldname EQ 'INCLUDE-NAME1'
      OR ls_fcat-fieldname EQ 'INCLUDE-CMT1' OR ls_fcat-fieldname EQ 'INCLUDE-CMT2'
      OR ls_fcat-fieldname EQ 'INCLUDE-CMT3' OR ls_fcat-fieldname EQ 'INCLUDE-CMT4' OR ls_fcat-fieldname EQ 'INCLUDE-CMT5'
      OR ls_fcat-fieldname EQ 'INCLUDE-ZYEAR1' OR ls_fcat-fieldname EQ 'INCLUDE-ZDATE1' OR ls_fcat-fieldname EQ 'INCLUDE-DESC1'
      OR ls_fcat-fieldname EQ 'INCLUDE-ZYEAR2' OR ls_fcat-fieldname EQ 'INCLUDE-ZDATE2' OR ls_fcat-fieldname EQ 'INCLUDE-DESC1'
      OR ls_fcat-fieldname EQ 'INCLUDE-ZYEAR3' OR ls_fcat-fieldname EQ 'INCLUDE-ZDATE3' OR ls_fcat-fieldname EQ 'INCLUDE-DESC1'
      OR ls_fcat-fieldname EQ 'INCLUDE-ZYEAR4' OR ls_fcat-fieldname EQ 'INCLUDE-ZDATE4' OR ls_fcat-fieldname EQ 'INCLUDE-DESC1'
      OR ls_fcat-fieldname EQ 'INCLUDE-ZYEAR5' OR ls_fcat-fieldname EQ 'INCLUDE-ZDATE5' OR ls_fcat-fieldname EQ 'INCLUDE-ZDATE6' OR ls_fcat-fieldname EQ 'INCLUDE-DESC1'
      OR ls_fcat-fieldname EQ 'INCLUDE-ZEVA_RESULT1' OR ls_fcat-fieldname EQ 'INCLUDE-ZMESSAGE1' OR ls_fcat-fieldname EQ 'INCLUDE-ZEVA_RESULT2' OR ls_fcat-fieldname EQ 'INCLUDE-ZMESSAGE2'
      OR ls_fcat-fieldname EQ 'INCLUDE-ZEVA_RESULT3' OR ls_fcat-fieldname EQ 'INCLUDE-ZMESSAGE3' OR ls_fcat-fieldname EQ 'INCLUDE-ZEVA_RESULT4' OR ls_fcat-fieldname EQ 'INCLUDE-ZMESSAGE4'
      OR ls_fcat-fieldname EQ 'INCLUDE-ZEVA_RESULT5' OR ls_fcat-fieldname EQ 'INCLUDE-ZMESSAGE5'.
    ELSE.
      ls_fcat-drdn_hndl = '1'.
      ls_fcat-drdn_alias = 'X'.
    ENDIF.

*    APPEND ls_fcat TO gt_fieldcat.
*    CLEAR ls_fcat.

    IF ls_fcat-fieldname = 'CHK'.
      ls_fcat-checkbox = abap_true.
    ELSE.
      ls_fcat-checkbox = abap_false.
    ENDIF.

    APPEND ls_fcat TO gt_fieldcat.
    CLEAR ls_fcat.

    IF p_arch IS INITIAL AND ( p_1 = 'SEQ' OR p_1 = 'AIND' OR p_1 = 'ADT' ).
      DELETE gt_fieldcat WHERE fieldname = p_1.
    ENDIF.

  ELSEIF rad6 = abap_true.
    wa_fcat-fieldname = p_1.
    wa_fcat-edit = p_2.
    wa_fcat-seltext_l = p_3.
    IF wa_fcat-fieldname = 'CHK'.
      wa_fcat-checkbox = abap_true.
    ELSE.
      wa_fcat-checkbox = abap_false.
    ENDIF.
    APPEND wa_fcat TO it_fcat.
    CLEAR wa_fcat.
* start 01.07.2023 | Aishwarya | SAPAMS-303 | MSDK909846 | archiving logic
    IF p_arch IS INITIAL AND ( p_1 = 'SEQ' OR p_1 = 'AIND' OR p_1 = 'ADT' ).
      DELETE it_fcat WHERE fieldname = p_1.
    ENDIF.
* end 01.07.2023 | Aishwarya | SAPAMS-303 | MSDK909846 | archiving logic

  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.

FORM display_data.
  wa_lay-colwidth_optimize = 'X'.
  IF rad6 = 'X'.
    CALL FUNCTION 'REUSE_ALV_GRID_DISPLAY'
      EXPORTING
        i_callback_program       = sy-cprog
        i_callback_pf_status_set = 'STATUS'
        i_callback_user_command  = 'USERCOMMAND'
        is_layout                = wa_lay
        it_fieldcat              = it_fcat
*       i_grid_title             = 'Vendor Evaluation Criteria'
        i_grid_title             = 'Vendor Performance Report'
      TABLES
        t_outtab                 = it_dat1
      EXCEPTIONS
        program_error            = 1
        OTHERS                   = 2.
  ELSE.
    CALL FUNCTION 'REUSE_ALV_GRID_DISPLAY'
      EXPORTING
        i_callback_program       = sy-cprog
        i_callback_pf_status_set = 'STATUS'
        i_callback_user_command  = 'USERCOMMAND'
        is_layout                = wa_lay
        it_fieldcat              = it_fcat
*       i_grid_title             = 'Vendor Evaluation Criteria'
        i_grid_title             = 'Vendor Evaluation for Endorsement'
      TABLES
        t_outtab                 = it_dat1
      EXCEPTIONS
        program_error            = 1
        OTHERS                   = 2.
  ENDIF.
  IF sy-subrc <> 0.
* Implement suitable error handling here
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      --> LV_DEPT
*&      --> LV_VAL
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM avg  USING    p_lv_dept
                   p_lv_val.
*  IF lv_calc IS INITIAL.
*    lv_calc = 1.
*  ENDIF.
*  CLEAR lv_wavg.
*  CONDENSE lv_calc NO-GAPS.
*  READ TABLE lt_dept INTO DATA(lw_dept) WITH KEY  dept = p_lv_dept.
*  IF sy-subrc EQ 0 AND lv_calc GT 0.
*    IF p_lv_dept = 'PROCUREMENT'.
*      lv_wavg = lw_dept-per /  lv_calc .
*    ELSE.
*      lv_wavg = lw_dept-per /  lv_calc.
*    ENDIF.
*  ENDIF.
**  IF lv_val ne wa_final-zlom or lv_val ne wa_final-zecm or lv_val ne wa_final-zSSRC.
*
*  READ TABLE lt_rat INTO DATA(lw_rat) WITH KEY rat1 = p_lv_val.
*  IF sy-subrc EQ 0.
*    lv_tot1 = ( lv_wavg * ( lw_rat-per / 100 ) ).
*    lv_tot = lv_tot + lv_tot1.
*  ELSE.
**    lw_rat-per = 50.
**    lv_tot1 = ( lv_wavg * ( lw_rat-per / 100 ) ).
**    lv_tot = lv_tot + lv_tot1.
*  ENDIF.
**  ENDIF.
*  IF p_lv_val EQ 'N/A'.
*    CLEAR p_lv_val.
*  ENDIF.

  IF lv_val NE 'N/A'.
    CLEAR lv_tot1.
    MOVE lv_val TO lv_tot1.
    lv_tot = lv_tot + lv_tot1.
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.

FORM status USING rt_extab TYPE slis_t_extab.
  DATA : lt_exclude TYPE TABLE OF ui_func.
  IF rad6 = 'X'.
    APPEND '&ENDORSE' TO lt_exclude.
    SET PF-STATUS 'ZSTATUS' EXCLUDING lt_exclude.
  ELSE.
    SET PF-STATUS 'ZSTATUS'.
  ENDIF.
ENDFORM.
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& Form save
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& text
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& -->  p1        text
*& <--  p2        text
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM save .


  DATA : lv_v1        TYPE char10,
         lv_v2        TYPE char10,
         lv_v3        TYPE char10,
         lv_v4        TYPE char10,
         lv_v5        TYPE char10,
         lv_tot2      TYPE char10,
         lv_total     TYPE p DECIMALS 2,
         ls_exch_rate TYPE bapi1093_0,
         lt_return    TYPE bapiret1.

  DATA lv_entries_are_consisted TYPE abap_bool.
  CLEAR : lv_entries_are_consisted.
  g_grid->check_changed_data(
    IMPORTING

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& Form fill_it_smf
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& text
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& -->  p1        text
*& <--  p2        text
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM fill_it_smf .
*  DATA : lv_ekpv TYPE char13,
*         lv_ekv  TYPE char13,
*         lv_ekv1 TYPE p DECIMALS 2.
*
*         select  partner,bpkind FROM but000 INTO TABLE @DATA(lt_but000) FOR ALL ENTRIES IN @it_dat WHERE partner = @it_dat-lifnr.
*  IF sy-subrc EQ 0.
*    SELECT bpkind,text40 FROM tb004t INTO TABLE @DATA(it_tb004t)
*      FOR ALL ENTRIES IN @lt_but000 WHERE bpkind = @lt_but000-bpkind AND spras = @sy-langu.
*  ENDIF.
*  SELECT lifnr,adrnr,stras,ort01,pstlz FROM lfa1 INTO TABLE @DATA(lt_adrnr) FOR ALL ENTRIES IN @it_dat WHERE lifnr = @it_dat-lifnr.
*
*  IF sy-subrc EQ 0.
*
*    SELECT  addrnumber,name1,name2,house_num1,city_code,po_box,post_code1,city1,tel_number,fax_number",Remark
*      FROM adrc
*      INTO TABLE @DATA(lt_add) FOR ALL ENTRIES IN @lt_adrnr WHERE addrnumber = @lt_adrnr-adrnr.
*    SELECT addrnumber,smtp_addr FROM adr6 INTO TABLE @DATA(lt_add6) FOR ALL ENTRIES IN @lt_adrnr
*       WHERE addrnumber = @lt_adrnr-adrnr.
*    SELECT addrnumber,remark FROM adrct INTO TABLE @DATA(lt_adrct) FOR ALL ENTRIES IN @lt_adrnr
*       WHERE addrnumber = @lt_adrnr-adrnr.
*  ENDIF.
*  SELECT * FROM  zvendor_mail  INTO TABLE @DATA(lt_mail) FOR ALL ENTRIES IN @it_dat WHERE lifnr = @it_dat-lifnr.
*
*  SELECT lifnr,ebeln,waers,wkurs FROM ekko
*                                 INTO TABLE @DATA(it_ekko)
*                                 FOR ALL ENTRIES IN @it_dat
*                                 WHERE lifnr = @it_dat-lifnr.
*  IF it_ekko IS NOT INITIAL.
*    SELECT ebeln,ebelp,netwr FROM ekpo
*                                INTO TABLE @DATA(it_ekpo)
*                                FOR ALL ENTRIES IN @it_ekko
*                                WHERE ebeln = @it_ekko-ebeln
*                                 AND loekz  NE 'L'.
*  ENDIF.
*
*
*
*
*  LOOP AT it_final INTO wa_final.
*    CLEAR wa_smf.
*    MOVE-CORRESPONDING wa_final TO wa_smf.
*
*    READ TABLE lt_adrnr INTO DATA(lw_adrnr) WITH KEY lifnr = wa_smf-lifnr.
*    IF sy-subrc EQ 0.
*      READ TABLE lt_adrct INTO DATA(lw_adrct) WITH KEY addrnumber = lw_adrnr-adrnr.
*      IF sy-subrc EQ 0.
*        wa_smf-zcd     =  lw_adrct-remark."lw_add-house_num1.
*      ENDIF.
*      READ TABLE lt_add INTO DATA(lw_add) WITH KEY addrnumber = lw_adrnr-adrnr.
*      IF sy-subrc EQ 0.
*        IF lw_add-name1 IS NOT INITIAL.
*          wa_smf-zcnam          =  lw_add-name1.
*        ELSEIF lw_add-name2 IS NOT INITIAL.
*          wa_smf-zcnam          =  lw_add-name2.
*        ENDIF.
*        wa_smf-house_num1     =  lw_adrnr-stras."lw_add-house_num1.
*        wa_smf-city_code      =  lw_adrnr-ort01."lw_add-city_code.
*        wa_smf-po_box         =  lw_adrnr-pstlz."lw_add-po_box.
*        wa_smf-post_code1     =  lw_add-post_code1.
*        wa_smf-city1          =  lw_add-city1 .
*        wa_smf-tel_number     =  lw_add-tel_number.
*        wa_smf-fax_number     =  lw_add-fax_number.
*        READ TABLE lt_but000 INTO DATA(lw_but000) WITH KEY partner = wa_smf-lifnr.
*        IF sy-subrc EQ 0.
*          READ TABLE it_tb004t INTO DATA(lw_tb004t) WITH KEY bpkind = lw_but000-bpkind.
*          wa_smf-bpkind     = lw_tb004t-text40 .
*        ENDIF.
*      ENDIF.
*      READ TABLE lt_add6 INTO DATA(lw_add6) WITH KEY addrnumber = lw_adrnr-adrnr.
*      IF sy-subrc EQ 0.
*        wa_smf-smtp_addr = lw_add6-smtp_addr.
*      ENDIF.
*      READ TABLE lt_mail INTO DATA(lw_mail) WITH KEY lifnr = wa_smf-lifnr..
*      IF sy-subrc EQ 0.
*        wa_smf-smtp_addr = lw_mail-mail.
*      ENDIF.
*    ENDIF.
*    LOOP AT it_ekko INTO DATA(wa_ekko) WHERE lifnr = wa_smf-lifnr.
*      LOOP AT it_ekpo INTO DATA(wa_ekpo) WHERE ebeln = wa_ekko-ebeln.
*        AT END OF ebeln.
*          DATA(l_fg) = abap_true.
*        ENDAT.
*        lv_ekpv = lv_ekpv + wa_ekpo-netwr .
*        IF l_fg = abap_true.
*          IF wa_ekko-waers NE 'USD'.
*            CONDENSE lv_ekpv NO-GAPS.
*            lv_ekv =  lv_ekpv * wa_ekko-wkurs.
*          ELSE.
*            lv_ekv  = lv_ekpv.
*          ENDIF.
*          CONDENSE lv_ekv NO-GAPS.
*          lv_ekv1 = lv_ekv.
*          wa_smf-tvly = wa_smf-tvly + lv_ekv.
*          CONDENSE wa_smf-tvly NO-GAPS.
*          CLEAR:lv_ekpv,lv_ekv,l_fg.
*        ENDIF.
*      ENDLOOP.
*    ENDLOOP.
*    wa_smf-tot = lv_tot.
*
*    APPEND wa_smf TO it_smf.
*  ENDLOOP.

ENDFORM.

        wa_f1-value = 'Disqualified'.
      ENDIF.
      APPEND wa_f1 TO it_f1.
      CLEAR wa_f1.

      wa_f1-descr = 'Evaluation Status'.
      wa_f1-value = 'Completed'.
      APPEND wa_f1 TO it_f1.
      CLEAR wa_f1.

    ELSEIF rad2 = abap_true.

      CLEAR : lv_v2.
      wa_f1-descr = 'Total Score Warehouse'.
      READ TABLE lt_data INTO wa_final WITH KEY lifnr = wa_final-lifnr seq = wa_final-seq.
      IF sy-subrc = 0.
        lv_calc = 3.
        IF wa_final-zdelh = 'N/A'.
          lv_calc = lv_calc - 1.
        ENDIF.
        IF wa_final-zds = 'N/A'.
          lv_calc = lv_calc - 1.
        ENDIF.
        IF wa_final-zdels = 'N/A'.
          lv_calc = lv_calc - 1.
        ENDIF.

        CLEAR lv_tot_dep.
        IF wa_final-zdelh  IS NOT INITIAL.
          lv_dept = 'WAREHOUSE'.
          lv_val  = wa_final-zdelh.
          IF lv_val NE 'N/A'.
            PERFORM avg USING lv_dept lv_val.
            lv_tot_dep = lv_tot_dep + lv_val.
          ENDIF.
        ENDIF.
        IF wa_final-zds  IS NOT INITIAL.
          lv_dept = 'WAREHOUSE'.
          lv_val  = wa_final-zds.
          IF lv_val NE 'N/A'.
            PERFORM avg USING lv_dept lv_val.
            lv_tot_dep = lv_tot_dep + lv_val.
          ENDIF.
        ENDIF.

        IF wa_final-zdels   IS NOT INITIAL.
          lv_dept = 'WAREHOUSE'.
          lv_val  = wa_final-zdels.
          IF lv_val NE 'N/A'.
            PERFORM avg USING lv_dept lv_val.
            lv_tot_dep = lv_tot_dep + lv_val.
          ENDIF.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
      lv_v2 = lv_tot_dep.
      CLEAR lv_tot_dep.
      CONDENSE lv_v2 NO-GAPS.
      IF lv_v2 IS NOT INITIAL.
        wa_f1-value = lv_v2.
      ENDIF.
      APPEND wa_f1 TO it_f1.
      CLEAR wa_f1.

      wa_f1-descr = 'Evaluation Result'.
      IF lv_v2 >= 7.
        wa_f1-value = 'Qualified'.
      ELSE.
        wa_f1-value = 'Disqualified'.
      ENDIF.
      APPEND wa_f1 TO it_f1.
      CLEAR wa_f1.

      wa_f1-descr = 'Evaluation Status'.
      wa_f1-value = 'Completed'.
      APPEND wa_f1 TO it_f1.
      CLEAR wa_f1.
    ELSEIF rad3 = abap_true.
      CLEAR lv_v3.
      wa_f1-descr = 'Total Score End-User'.
      READ TABLE lt_data INTO wa_final WITH KEY lifnr = wa_final-lifnr seq = wa_final-seq.
      IF sy-subrc = 0.
        SELECT SINGLE ekgrp, eknam FROM t024 INTO @DATA(lw_ekgrp)
 WHERE eknam = @lv_eknam.
        wa_final-ekgrp1 = lw_ekgrp-ekgrp.
        wa_final-desc1 = lw_ekgrp-eknam.

        lv_calc = 3.
        IF wa_final-zpq = 'N/A'.
          lv_calc = lv_calc - 1.
        ENDIF.
        IF wa_final-zma = 'N/A'.
          lv_calc = lv_calc - 1.
        ENDIF.
        IF wa_final-zas = 'N/A'.
          lv_calc = lv_calc - 1.
        ENDIF.

        CLEAR lv_tot_dep.
        IF wa_final-zpq  IS NOT INITIAL.
          lv_dept = 'END-USER'.

          lv_val  = wa_final-zpq.
          IF lv_val NE 'N/A'.
            PERFORM avg USING lv_dept lv_val.
            lv_tot_dep = lv_tot_dep + lv_val.
          ENDIF.
        ENDIF.
        IF wa_final-zma   IS NOT INITIAL.
          lv_dept = 'END-USER'.
          lv_val  = wa_final-zma.
          IF lv_val NE 'N/A'.
            PERFORM avg USING lv_dept lv_val.
            lv_tot_dep = lv_tot_dep + lv_val.
          ENDIF.
        ENDIF.
        IF wa_final-zas   IS NOT INITIAL.
          lv_dept = 'END-USER'.
          lv_val  = wa_final-zas.
          IF lv_val NE 'N/A'.
            PERFORM avg USING lv_dept lv_val.
            lv_tot_dep = lv_tot_dep + lv_val.
          ENDIF.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
      lv_v3 = lv_tot_dep.
      CLEAR lv_tot_dep.
      CONDENSE lv_v3 NO-GAPS.
      IF lv_v3 IS NOT INITIAL.
        wa_f1-value = lv_v3.
      ENDIF.
      APPEND wa_f1 TO it_f1.
      CLEAR wa_f1.

      wa_f1-descr = 'Evaluation Result'.
      IF lv_v3 >= 7.
        wa_f1-value = 'Qualified'.
      ELSE.
        wa_f1-value = 'Disqualified'.
      ENDIF.
      APPEND wa_f1 TO it_f1.
      CLEAR wa_f1.

      wa_f1-descr = 'Evaluation Status'.
      wa_f1-value = 'Completed'.
      APPEND wa_f1 TO it_f1.
      CLEAR wa_f1.

    ELSEIF rad4 = abap_true.
      CLEAR : lv_v4.
      wa_f1-descr = 'Total Score Accounts'.
      READ TABLE lt_data INTO wa_final WITH KEY lifnr = wa_final-lifnr seq = wa_final-seq.
      IF sy-subrc = 0.
        lv_calc = 3.
        IF wa_final-zdoc = 'N/A'.
          lv_calc = lv_calc - 1.
        ENDIF.
        IF wa_final-znpi = 'N/A'.
          lv_calc = lv_calc - 1.
        ENDIF.
        IF wa_final-zsi = 'N/A'.
          lv_calc = lv_calc - 1.
        ENDIF.

        CLEAR lv_tot_dep.

        IF wa_final-zdoc   IS NOT INITIAL.
          lv_dept = 'ACCOUNTING'.

          lv_val  = wa_final-zdoc.
          IF lv_val NE 'N/A'.
            PERFORM avg USING lv_dept lv_val.
            lv_tot_dep = lv_tot_dep + lv_val.
          ENDIF.
        ENDIF.
        IF wa_final-znpi   IS NOT INITIAL.
          lv_dept = 'ACCOUNTING'.
          lv_val  = wa_final-znpi.
          IF lv_val NE 'N/A'.
            PERFORM avg USING lv_dept lv_val.
            lv_tot_dep = lv_tot_dep + lv_val.
          ENDIF.
        ENDIF.
        IF wa_final-zsi   IS NOT INITIAL.
          lv_dept = 'ACCOUNTING'.
          lv_val  = wa_final-zsi.
          IF lv_val NE 'N/A'.
            PERFORM avg USING lv_dept lv_val.
            lv_tot_dep = lv_tot_dep + lv_val.
          ENDIF.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
      lv_v4 = lv_tot_dep.
      CLEAR lv_tot_dep.
      CONDENSE lv_v4 NO-GAPS.
      IF lv_v4 IS NOT INITIAL.
        wa_f1-value = lv_v4.
      ENDIF.
      APPEND wa_f1 TO it_f1.
      CLEAR wa_f1.

      wa_f1-descr = 'Evaluation Result'.
      IF lv_v4 >= 7.
        wa_f1-value = 'Qualified'.
      ELSE.
        wa_f1-value = 'Disqualified'.
      ENDIF.
      APPEND wa_f1 TO it_f1.
      CLEAR wa_f1.

      wa_f1-descr = 'Evaluation Status'.
      wa_f1-value = 'Completed'.
      APPEND wa_f1 TO it_f1.
      CLEAR wa_f1.

    ELSEIF rad5 = abap_true.

      CLEAR :lv_v5.
      wa_f1-descr = 'Total Score EHS'.

      lv_calc = 4.
      IF wa_final-zscdh = 'N/A'.
        lv_calc = lv_calc - 1.
      ENDIF.
      IF wa_final-zlom = 'N/A'.
        lv_calc = lv_calc - 1.
      ENDIF.
      IF wa_final-zecm = 'N/A'.
        lv_calc = lv_calc - 1.
      ENDIF.
      IF wa_final-zssrc = 'N/A'.
        lv_calc = lv_calc - 1.
      ENDIF.

      CLEAR lv_tot_dep.

      IF wa_final-zscdh   IS NOT INITIAL.
        lv_dept = 'EHS'.
        lv_val  = wa_final-zscdh.
        PERFORM avg USING lv_dept lv_val.
        IF lv_val <> 'N/A'.
          lv_tot_dep = lv_tot_dep + lv_val.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
      IF wa_final-zlom   IS NOT INITIAL.
        lv_dept = 'EHS'.
        lv_val  = wa_final-zlom.
        PERFORM avg USING lv_dept lv_val.
        IF lv_val <> 'N/A'.
          lv_tot_dep = lv_tot_dep + lv_val.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
      IF wa_final-zecm   IS NOT INITIAL.
        lv_dept = 'EHS'.
        lv_val  = wa_final-zecm.
        PERFORM avg USING lv_dept lv_val.
        IF lv_val <> 'N/A'.
          lv_tot_dep = lv_tot_dep + lv_val.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
      IF wa_final-zssrc   IS NOT INITIAL.
        lv_dept = 'EHS'.
        lv_val  = wa_final-zssrc.
        PERFORM avg USING lv_dept lv_val.
        IF lv_val <> 'N/A'.
          lv_tot_dep = lv_tot_dep + lv_val.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
      lv_v5 = lv_tot_dep.
      CLEAR lv_tot_dep.
      CONDENSE lv_v5 NO-GAPS.
      IF lv_v5 IS NOT INITIAL.
        wa_f1-value = lv_v5.
      ENDIF.
      APPEND wa_f1 TO it_f1.
      CLEAR wa_f1.

      wa_f1-descr = 'Evaluation Result'.
      IF lv_v5 >= 10.
        wa_f1-value = 'Qualified'.
      ELSE.
        wa_f1-value = 'Disqualified'.
      ENDIF.
      APPEND wa_f1 TO it_f1.
      CLEAR wa_f1.

      wa_f1-descr = 'Evaluation Status'.
      wa_f1-value = 'Completed'.
      APPEND wa_f1 TO it_f1.
      CLEAR wa_f1.

    ENDIF.
  ENDLOOP.


ENDFORM.

  DATA : g_rows TYPE lvc_t_row,
         g_id   TYPE lvc_t_roid.

    IMPORTING

      et_row_no     = g_id.

  ENDIF.

        wa_final-zds = <fs_fin>-include-zds.

      IF  <fs_fin>-include-zdels IS NOT INITIAL.
        wa_final-zdels = <fs_fin>-include-zdels.
      ENDIF.
      IF  <fs_fin>-include-zpq IS NOT INITIAL.
        wa_final-zpq = <fs_fin>-include-zpq.
      ENDIF.
      IF  <fs_fin>-include-zma IS NOT INITIAL.
        wa_final-zma = <fs_fin>-include-zma.

    IF sy-subrc = 0.

  LOOP AT it_final INTO wa_final.

    TYPES:BEGIN OF ty_f1,

      IF wa_final-zdelp  = 'N/A'.
        lv_calc = lv_calc - 1.
      ENDIF.
      IF wa_final-zpprc = 'N/A'.
        lv_calc = lv_calc - 1.
      ENDIF.
      IF wa_final-zcc = 'N/A'.
        lv_calc = lv_calc - 1.
      ENDIF.
      IF wa_final-zpt = 'N/A'.
        lv_calc = lv_calc - 1.
      ENDIF.
      IF wa_final-zar = 'N/A'.
        lv_calc = lv_calc - 1.

      IF wa_final-zr = 'N/A'.

      ENDIF.

        lv_dept = 'PROCUREMENT'.
        lv_val  = wa_final-zstk.
        IF lv_val NE 'N/A'.
          PERFORM avg USING lv_dept lv_val.
          lv_tot_dep = lv_tot_dep + lv_val.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
      IF wa_final-zdelp   IS NOT INITIAL.
        lv_dept = 'PROCUREMENT'.
        lv_val  = wa_final-zdelp.
        IF lv_val NE 'N/A'.
          PERFORM avg USING lv_dept lv_val.
          lv_tot_dep = lv_tot_dep + lv_val.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
      IF wa_final-zpprc   IS NOT INITIAL.
        lv_dept = 'PROCUREMENT'.
        lv_val  = wa_final-zpprc.
        IF lv_val NE 'N/A'.
          PERFORM avg USING lv_dept lv_val.
          lv_tot_dep = lv_tot_dep + lv_val.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
      IF wa_final-zcc   IS NOT INITIAL.
        lv_dept = 'PROCUREMENT'.
        lv_val  = wa_final-zcc.
        IF lv_val NE 'N/A'.
          PERFORM avg USING lv_dept lv_val.
          lv_tot_dep = lv_tot_dep + lv_val.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
      IF wa_final-zpt   IS NOT INITIAL.
        lv_dept = 'PROCUREMENT'.
        lv_val  = wa_final-zpt.
        IF lv_val NE 'N/A'.
          PERFORM avg USING lv_dept lv_val.
          lv_tot_dep = lv_tot_dep + lv_val.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
      IF wa_final-zar   IS NOT INITIAL.
        lv_dept = 'PROCUREMENT'.
        lv_val  = wa_final-zar.
        IF lv_val NE 'N/A'.
          PERFORM avg USING lv_dept lv_val.
          lv_tot_dep = lv_tot_dep + lv_val.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
      IF wa_final-zr   IS NOT INITIAL.
        lv_dept = 'PROCUREMENT'.
        lv_val  = wa_final-zr.
        IF lv_val NE 'N/A'.
          PERFORM avg USING lv_dept lv_val.
          lv_tot_dep = lv_tot_dep + lv_val.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.

      lv_v1 = lv_tot_dep.
      CLEAR lv_tot_dep.

      CONDENSE lv_v1 NO-GAPS.
      IF lv_v1 IS NOT INITIAL.
        IF lv_v1 >= 17.
          wa_f1-value = 'Qualified'.
        ELSE.
          wa_f1-value = 'Disqualified'.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.

    ELSEIF rad2 = abap_true.

      CLEAR : lv_v2.
      wa_f1-descr = 'Total Score Warehouse'.
      READ TABLE lt_data INTO wa_final WITH KEY lifnr = wa_final-lifnr seq = wa_final-seq.
      IF sy-subrc = 0.
        lv_calc = 3.
        IF wa_final-zdelh = 'N/A'.
          lv_calc = lv_calc - 1.
        ENDIF.
        IF wa_final-zds = 'N/A'.
          lv_calc = lv_calc - 1.
        ENDIF.
        IF wa_final-zdels = 'N/A'.
          lv_calc = lv_calc - 1.
        ENDIF.

        CLEAR lv_tot_dep.
        IF wa_final-zdelh  IS NOT INITIAL.
          lv_dept = 'WAREHOUSE'.
          lv_val  = wa_final-zdelh.
          IF lv_val NE 'N/A'.
            PERFORM avg USING lv_dept lv_val.
            lv_tot_dep = lv_tot_dep + lv_val.
          ENDIF.
        ENDIF.
        IF wa_final-zds  IS NOT INITIAL.
          lv_dept = 'WAREHOUSE'.
          lv_val  = wa_final-zds.
          IF lv_val NE 'N/A'.
            PERFORM avg USING lv_dept lv_val.
            lv_tot_dep = lv_tot_dep + lv_val.
          ENDIF.
        ENDIF.

        IF wa_final-zdels   IS NOT INITIAL.
          lv_dept = 'WAREHOUSE'.
          lv_val  = wa_final-zdels.
          IF lv_val NE 'N/A'.
            PERFORM avg USING lv_dept lv_val.
            lv_tot_dep = lv_tot_dep + lv_val.
          ENDIF.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
      lv_v2 = lv_tot_dep.
      CLEAR lv_tot_dep.
      CONDENSE lv_v2 NO-GAPS.
      IF lv_v2 IS NOT INITIAL.
        IF lv_v2 >= 7.
          wa_f1-value = 'Qualified'.
        ELSE.
          wa_f1-value = 'Disqualified'.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.

    ELSEIF rad3 = abap_true.

      CLEAR lv_v3.
      wa_f1-descr = 'Total Score End-User'.
      READ TABLE lt_data INTO wa_final WITH KEY lifnr = wa_final-lifnr seq = wa_final-seq.
      IF sy-subrc = 0.
        SELECT SINGLE ekgrp, eknam FROM t024 INTO @DATA(lw_ekgrp)
 WHERE eknam = @lv_eknam.
        wa_final-ekgrp1 = lw_ekgrp-ekgrp.
        wa_final-desc1 = lw_ekgrp-eknam.

        lv_calc = 3.
        IF wa_final-zpq = 'N/A'.
          lv_calc = lv_calc - 1.
        ENDIF.
        IF wa_final-zma = 'N/A'.
          lv_calc = lv_calc - 1.
        ENDIF.
        IF wa_final-zas = 'N/A'.
          lv_calc = lv_calc - 1.
        ENDIF.

        CLEAR lv_tot_dep.
        IF wa_final-zpq  IS NOT INITIAL.
          lv_dept = 'END-USER'.

          lv_val  = wa_final-zpq.
          IF lv_val NE 'N/A'.
            PERFORM avg USING lv_dept lv_val.
            lv_tot_dep = lv_tot_dep + lv_val.
          ENDIF.
        ENDIF.
        IF wa_final-zma   IS NOT INITIAL.
          lv_dept = 'END-USER'.
          lv_val  = wa_final-zma.
          IF lv_val NE 'N/A'.
            PERFORM avg USING lv_dept lv_val.
            lv_tot_dep = lv_tot_dep + lv_val.
          ENDIF.
        ENDIF.
        IF wa_final-zas   IS NOT INITIAL.
          lv_dept = 'END-USER'.
          lv_val  = wa_final-zas.
          IF lv_val NE 'N/A'.
            PERFORM avg USING lv_dept lv_val.
            lv_tot_dep = lv_tot_dep + lv_val.
          ENDIF.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
      lv_v3 = lv_tot_dep.
      CLEAR lv_tot_dep.
      CONDENSE lv_v3 NO-GAPS.
      IF lv_v3 IS NOT INITIAL.
        IF lv_v3 >= 7.
          wa_f1-value = 'Qualified'.
        ELSE.
          wa_f1-value = 'Disqualified'.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.

    ELSEIF rad4 = abap_true.

      CLEAR : lv_v4.
      wa_f1-descr = 'Total Score Accounts'.
      READ TABLE lt_data INTO wa_final WITH KEY lifnr = wa_final-lifnr seq = wa_final-seq.
      IF sy-subrc = 0.
        lv_calc = 3.
        IF wa_final-zdoc = 'N/A'.
          lv_calc = lv_calc - 1.
        ENDIF.
        IF wa_final-znpi = 'N/A'.
          lv_calc = lv_calc - 1.
        ENDIF.
        IF wa_final-zsi = 'N/A'.
          lv_calc = lv_calc - 1.
        ENDIF.

        CLEAR lv_tot_dep.

        IF wa_final-zdoc   IS NOT INITIAL.
          lv_dept = 'ACCOUNTING'.

          lv_val  = wa_final-zdoc.
          IF lv_val NE 'N/A'.
            PERFORM avg USING lv_dept lv_val.
            lv_tot_dep = lv_tot_dep + lv_val.
          ENDIF.
        ENDIF.
        IF wa_final-znpi   IS NOT INITIAL.
          lv_dept = 'ACCOUNTING'.
          lv_val  = wa_final-znpi.
          IF lv_val NE 'N/A'.
            PERFORM avg USING lv_dept lv_val.
            lv_tot_dep = lv_tot_dep + lv_val.
          ENDIF.
        ENDIF.
        IF wa_final-zsi   IS NOT INITIAL.
          lv_dept = 'ACCOUNTING'.
          lv_val  = wa_final-zsi.
          IF lv_val NE 'N/A'.
            PERFORM avg USING lv_dept lv_val.
            lv_tot_dep = lv_tot_dep + lv_val.
          ENDIF.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
      lv_v4 = lv_tot_dep.
      CLEAR lv_tot_dep.
      CONDENSE lv_v4 NO-GAPS.
      IF lv_v4 IS NOT INITIAL.
        IF lv_v4 >= 7.
          wa_f1-value = 'Qualified'.
        ELSE.
          wa_f1-value = 'Disqualified'.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.

    ELSEIF rad5 = abap_true.

      CLEAR :lv_v5.
      wa_f1-descr = 'Total Score EHS'.

      lv_calc = 4.
      IF wa_final-zscdh = 'N/A'.
        lv_calc = lv_calc - 1.
      ENDIF.
      IF wa_final-zlom = 'N/A'.
        lv_calc = lv_calc - 1.
      ENDIF.
      IF wa_final-zecm = 'N/A'.
        lv_calc = lv_calc - 1.
      ENDIF.
      IF wa_final-zssrc = 'N/A'.
        lv_calc = lv_calc - 1.
      ENDIF.

      CLEAR lv_tot_dep.

      IF wa_final-zscdh   IS NOT INITIAL.
        lv_dept = 'EHS'.
        lv_val  = wa_final-zscdh.
        PERFORM avg USING lv_dept lv_val.
        IF lv_val <> 'N/A'.
          lv_tot_dep = lv_tot_dep + lv_val.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
      IF wa_final-zlom   IS NOT INITIAL.
        lv_dept = 'EHS'.
        lv_val  = wa_final-zlom.
        PERFORM avg USING lv_dept lv_val.
        IF lv_val <> 'N/A'.
          lv_tot_dep = lv_tot_dep + lv_val.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
      IF wa_final-zecm   IS NOT INITIAL.
        lv_dept = 'EHS'.
        lv_val  = wa_final-zecm.
        PERFORM avg USING lv_dept lv_val.
        IF lv_val <> 'N/A'.
          lv_tot_dep = lv_tot_dep + lv_val.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
      IF wa_final-zssrc   IS NOT INITIAL.
        lv_dept = 'EHS'.
        lv_val  = wa_final-zssrc.
        PERFORM avg USING lv_dept lv_val.
        IF lv_val <> 'N/A'.
          lv_tot_dep = lv_tot_dep + lv_val.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
      lv_v5 = lv_tot_dep.
      CLEAR lv_tot_dep.
      CONDENSE lv_v5 NO-GAPS.
      IF lv_v5 IS NOT INITIAL.
        IF lv_v5 >= 10.
          wa_f1-value = 'Qualified'.
        ELSE.
          wa_f1-value = 'Disqualified'.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
  ENDLOOP.

ENDFORM.




FORM endorsement_operations1.

  DATA: gd_repid              LIKE sy-repid, "Exists
        ref_grid              TYPE REF TO cl_gui_alv_grid,
        lv_fnam               TYPE rs38l_fnam,
        w_compop              TYPE ssfcompop,
        t_otf                 TYPE itcoo OCCURS 0 WITH HEADER LINE,
        t_otf_from_fm         TYPE ssfcrescl,
        t_pdf_tab             LIKE tline OCCURS 0 WITH HEADER LINE,
        lv_control_parameters TYPE ssfctrlop,
        w_bin_filesize        TYPE i, " Binary File Size
        w_file_name           TYPE string,
        w_file_path           TYPE string,
        gv_devtype            TYPE rspoptype,
        w_full_path           TYPE string,
        lv_ekpv               TYPE char13,
        lv_ekv                TYPE char13,
        lv_ekv1               TYPE p DECIMALS 2,
        c_check               TYPE char1 VALUE 'X',
        ls_exch_rate          TYPE bapi1093_0,
        lt_return             TYPE bapiret1,
        rs_selfield           TYPE slis_selfield.
  REFRESH:it_smf.
  IF ref_grid IS INITIAL.
    CALL FUNCTION 'GET_GLOBALS_FROM_SLVC_FULLSCR'
      IMPORTING
        e_grid = ref_grid.
  ENDIF.
*  CALL METHOD g_grid->refresh_table_display.
  IF ref_grid IS NOT INITIAL.
    CALL METHOD ref_grid->check_changed_data.
  ENDIF.

  DATA : g_rows TYPE lvc_t_row,
         g_id   TYPE lvc_t_roid.
  SORT it_final BY lifnr.
  CALL METHOD g_grid->get_selected_rows
    IMPORTING
      et_index_rows = g_rows
      et_row_no     = g_id.

  SELECT partner,bpkind FROM but000 INTO TABLE @DATA(lt_but000) FOR ALL ENTRIES IN @it_dat WHERE partner = @it_dat-lifnr.
  IF sy-subrc EQ 0.
    SELECT bpkind,text40 FROM tb004t INTO TABLE @DATA(it_tb004t)
      FOR ALL ENTRIES IN @lt_but000 WHERE bpkind = @lt_but000-bpkind AND spras = @sy-langu.
  ENDIF.
  SELECT lifnr,adrnr,stras,ort01,pstlz FROM lfa1 INTO TABLE @DATA(lt_adrnr) FOR ALL ENTRIES IN @it_dat WHERE lifnr = @it_dat-lifnr.

  IF sy-subrc EQ 0.

    SELECT  addrnumber,name1,name2,street, house_num1,city_code,po_box,post_code1,city1,tel_number,fax_number,country"",Remark
      FROM adrc
      INTO TABLE @DATA(lt_add) FOR ALL ENTRIES IN @lt_adrnr WHERE addrnumber = @lt_adrnr-adrnr.
    IF lt_add IS NOT INITIAL.
      SELECT land1 , landx FROM t005t INTO TABLE @DATA(lt_t005) FOR ALL ENTRIES IN @lt_add WHERE
        spras = 'E' AND land1 = @lt_add-country.
    ENDIF.
    SELECT addrnumber,smtp_addr FROM adr6 INTO TABLE @DATA(lt_add6) FOR ALL ENTRIES IN @lt_adrnr
       WHERE addrnumber = @lt_adrnr-adrnr.
    SELECT addrnumber,remark FROM adrct INTO TABLE @DATA(lt_adrct) FOR ALL ENTRIES IN @lt_adrnr
       WHERE addrnumber = @lt_adrnr-adrnr.
  ENDIF.
  SELECT * FROM  zvendor_mail  INTO TABLE @DATA(lt_mail) FOR ALL ENTRIES IN @it_dat WHERE lifnr = @it_dat-lifnr.

  SELECT lifnr,ebeln,waers,wkurs FROM ekko
                                 INTO TABLE @DATA(it_ekko)
                                 FOR ALL ENTRIES IN @it_dat
                                 WHERE lifnr = @it_dat-lifnr.
  IF it_ekko IS NOT INITIAL.
    SELECT ebeln,ebelp,netwr FROM ekpo
                                INTO TABLE @DATA(it_ekpo)
                                FOR ALL ENTRIES IN @it_ekko
                                WHERE ebeln = @it_ekko-ebeln
                                 AND loekz  NE 'L'.
  ENDIF.

  CLEAR: wa_smf.

  LOOP AT it_dat1 INTO wa_dat1 WHERE chk = 'X'.
    CALL FUNCTION 'CONVERSION_EXIT_ALPHA_INPUT'
      EXPORTING
        input  = wa_dat1-lifnr
      IMPORTING
        output = wa_dat1-lifnr.
    .
    READ TABLE it_dat INTO wa_dat WITH KEY lifnr = wa_dat1-lifnr.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.

      MOVE-CORRESPONDING wa_dat TO wa_smf.

      READ TABLE lt_data INTO wa_data WITH KEY ekgrp1 = wa_dat-ekgrp.
      IF sy-subrc = 0.
        wa_smf-ekgrp1 = wa_data-ekgrp1.
      ENDIF.

      DATA : lv_lifnr TYPE lifnr.
      lv_lifnr = wa_smf-lifnr.

      READ TABLE lt_data INTO wa_data WITH KEY lifnr = lv_lifnr.
      IF sy-subrc = 0.
        MOVE-CORRESPONDING wa_data TO wa_smf.
      ENDIF.


      READ TABLE lt_adrnr INTO DATA(lw_adrnr) WITH KEY lifnr = wa_smf-lifnr.
      IF sy-subrc EQ 0.
        READ TABLE lt_adrct INTO DATA(lw_adrct) WITH KEY addrnumber = lw_adrnr-adrnr.
        IF sy-subrc EQ 0.
          wa_smf-zcd     =  lw_adrct-remark."lw_add-house_num1.
        ENDIF.
        READ TABLE lt_add INTO DATA(lw_add) WITH KEY addrnumber = lw_adrnr-adrnr.
        IF sy-subrc EQ 0.
          IF lw_add-name1 IS NOT INITIAL.
            wa_smf-zcnam          =  lw_add-name1.
          ELSEIF lw_add-name2 IS NOT INITIAL.
            wa_smf-zcnam          =  lw_add-name2.
          ENDIF.
          wa_smf-house_num1     =  lw_add-street. "MPCLAMS-T1248 21.01.2024
          wa_smf-post_code1     =  lw_add-post_code1. "MPCLAMS-T1248 21.01.2024
          wa_smf-city1          =  lw_add-city1 . "MPCLAMS-T1248 21.01.2024
          wa_smf-city_code      =  lw_adrnr-ort01."lw_add-city_code. "28.12.2023 | Aishwarya | MPCLAMS-243 | MSDK910164 TR MPCLAMS-225
          wa_smf-po_box         =  lw_adrnr-pstlz."lw_add-po_box. "28.12.2023 | Aishwarya | MPCLAMS-243 | MSDK910164 TR MPCLAMS-225
          wa_smf-tel_number     =  lw_add-tel_number.
          wa_smf-fax_number     =  lw_add-fax_number.
          READ TABLE lt_t005 ASSIGNING FIELD-SYMBOL(<fs_t005>) WITH KEY land1 = lw_add-country. "28.12.2023 | Aishwarya | MPCLAMS-243 | MSDK910164 TR MPCLAMS-225
          "MPCLAMS-T1248 21.01.2024 START
          wa_smf-postcode_city = lw_add-city1.
          IF <fs_t005> IS ASSIGNED.
            IF wa_smf-postcode_city IS NOT INITIAL.
              CONCATENATE wa_smf-postcode_city <fs_t005>-landx INTO wa_smf-postcode_city SEPARATED BY ', '.
            ELSE.
              wa_smf-postcode_city = <fs_t005>-landx.
            ENDIF.
          ENDIF.
          IF wa_smf-post_code1 IS NOT INITIAL.
            IF wa_smf-postcode_city IS NOT INITIAL.
              CONCATENATE wa_smf-post_code1 wa_smf-postcode_city INTO wa_smf-postcode_city SEPARATED BY ', '.
            ELSE.
              wa_smf-postcode_city = wa_smf-post_code1.
            ENDIF.
          ENDIF.
          "MPCLAMS-T1248 21.01.2024 END
          READ TABLE lt_but000 INTO DATA(lw_but000) WITH KEY partner = wa_smf-lifnr.
          IF sy-subrc EQ 0.
            READ TABLE it_tb004t INTO DATA(lw_tb004t) WITH KEY bpkind = lw_but000-bpkind.
            wa_smf-bpkind     = lw_tb004t-text40 .
          ENDIF.
        ENDIF.
        READ TABLE lt_add6 INTO DATA(lw_add6) WITH KEY addrnumber = lw_adrnr-adrnr.
        IF sy-subrc EQ 0.
          wa_smf-smtp_addr = lw_add6-smtp_addr.
        ENDIF.
        READ TABLE lt_mail INTO DATA(lw_mail) WITH KEY lifnr = wa_smf-lifnr..
        IF sy-subrc EQ 0.
          wa_smf-smtp_addr = lw_mail-mail.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.


      LOOP AT it_ekko INTO DATA(wa_ekko) WHERE lifnr = wa_smf-lifnr.
        LOOP AT it_ekpo INTO DATA(wa_ekpo) WHERE ebeln = wa_ekko-ebeln.
          AT END OF ebeln.
            DATA(l_fg) = abap_true.
          ENDAT.
          lv_ekpv = lv_ekpv + wa_ekpo-netwr .
          IF l_fg = abap_true.
            IF wa_ekko-waers NE 'USD'.

              CALL FUNCTION 'BAPI_EXCHANGERATE_GETDETAIL'
                EXPORTING
                  rate_type  = 'M'
                  from_curr  = wa_ekko-waers
                  to_currncy = 'USD'
                  date       = sy-datum
                IMPORTING
                  exch_rate  = ls_exch_rate
                  return     = lt_return.
              wa_ekko-wkurs = ls_exch_rate-exch_rate.

              CONDENSE lv_ekpv NO-GAPS.
              lv_ekv =  lv_ekpv * wa_ekko-wkurs.
            ELSE.
              lv_ekv  = lv_ekpv.
            ENDIF.
            CONDENSE lv_ekv NO-GAPS.
            lv_ekv1 = lv_ekv.
            wa_smf-tvly = wa_smf-tvly + lv_ekv.
            CONDENSE wa_smf-tvly NO-GAPS.
            CLEAR:lv_ekpv,lv_ekv,l_fg.
          ENDIF.
        ENDLOOP.
      ENDLOOP.
      wa_smf-tot = wa_dat1-total.
      CONDENSE wa_smf-tot NO-GAPS.

      lv_ekv1 = wa_smf-tvly.
      wa_smf-tvly = lv_ekv1.
      APPEND wa_smf TO it_smf.
      CLEAR wa_smf.

    ENDIF.
  ENDLOOP.


  CASE sy-ucomm.
    WHEN 'BACK' OR 'EXIT' OR 'CANCEL'.
      LEAVE TO SCREEN '0'.

    WHEN '&ZALL'.

      LOOP AT it_dat1_new INTO wa_dat1_new WHERE chk NE 'X' .
        wa_dat1_new-chk = 'X'.
        MODIFY it_dat1_new FROM wa_dat1_new TRANSPORTING chk.
      ENDLOOP.
      rs_selfield-refresh = abap_true.


    WHEN '&ZSAL'.
      LOOP AT it_dat1_new INTO wa_dat1_new WHERE chk = 'X' .
        wa_dat1_new-chk = ' '.
        MODIFY it_dat1_new FROM wa_dat1_new TRANSPORTING chk.
      ENDLOOP.
*      rs_selfield-refresh = c_check.




    WHEN '&ENDORSE'.
      REFRESH : it_dat1.

      LOOP AT it_dat1_new INTO wa_dat1_new.
        SELECT SINGLE * FROM ztb_ven_eva INTO @DATA(it_ztb_ven_evaa) WHERE lifnr = @wa_dat1_new-lifnr AND seq = @wa_dat1_new-seq.
        IF sy-subrc = 0.
          IF it_ztb_ven_evaa-zend_submit = 'X'.
            MESSAGE 'Endorsement already done' TYPE 'E'.
            LEAVE TO SCREEN 600.
          ENDIF.
        ENDIF.
      ENDLOOP.

      LOOP AT it_dat1_new ASSIGNING FIELD-SYMBOL(<fs_fin>).
        APPEND INITIAL LINE TO  it_dat1 ASSIGNING FIELD-SYMBOL(<fs_fin1>).
        IF <fs_fin1> IS ASSIGNED.
          <fs_fin1> = <fs_fin>.
          <fs_fin1>-lifnr = |{ <fs_fin1>-lifnr ALPHA = IN }|.
          SELECT SINGLE * FROM ztb_ven_eva INTO @DATA(it_ztb_ven_eva) WHERE lifnr = @<fs_fin1>-lifnr AND seq = @<fs_fin1>-seq.
          IF sy-subrc = 0.
            it_ztb_ven_eva-usr6 = sy-uname.
            it_ztb_ven_eva-zdate6 = sy-datum.
            <fs_fin>-zdate6 = sy-datum.
            MODIFY ztb_ven_eva FROM @it_ztb_ven_eva.
            COMMIT WORK.
*            PERFORM send_mail1.
            PERFORM notif_mail1.
          ELSE.
*            MESSAGE 'Please save the result then submit' TYPE 'E'.
          ENDIF.
        ENDIF.
      ENDLOOP.


    WHEN 'PDFDOWNLOA'.

      CLEAR: lv_fnam,t_otf_from_fm.
      CALL FUNCTION 'SSF_FUNCTION_MODULE_NAME'
        EXPORTING
          formname           = 'ZMM_SF_VEN_EVA1'
*         VARIANT            = ' '
*         DIRECT_CALL        = ' '
        IMPORTING
          fm_name            = lv_fnam
        EXCEPTIONS
          no_form            = 1
          no_function_module = 2
          OTHERS             = 3.
      IF sy-subrc <> 0.
* Implement suitable error handling here
      ENDIF.
******************************************************
      CALL FUNCTION 'SSF_GET_DEVICE_TYPE'
        EXPORTING
          i_language             = sy-langu
        IMPORTING
          e_devtype              = gv_devtype
        EXCEPTIONS
          no_language            = 1
          language_not_installed = 2
          no_devtype_found       = 3
          system_error           = 4
          OTHERS                 = 5.
      IF sy-subrc <> 0.
* Implement suitable error handling here
      ENDIF.

      DATA : gv_username5 TYPE bapibname-bapibname.
      DATA : gs_defaults TYPE bapidefaul.   "SPLD
      DATA : gt_return TYPE TABLE OF bapiret2.
      DATA : ls_job_output   TYPE ssfcrescl,
             lc_file         TYPE string,
             lt_lines        TYPE TABLE OF tline,
             li_pdf_fsize    TYPE i,
             ls_pdf_string_x TYPE xstring,
             ls_pdf          TYPE char80,
             lt_pdf          TYPE TABLE OF char80.

      gv_username5 = sy-uname.
      CALL FUNCTION 'BAPI_USER_GET_DETAIL'
        EXPORTING
          username = gv_username5
        IMPORTING
          defaults = gs_defaults
        TABLES
          return   = gt_return.
      CLEAR : w_compop, lv_control_parameters.
      w_compop-tdimmed = abap_true.
      w_compop-tdnewid = abap_true.
      w_compop-tddest =  gs_defaults-spld.
      w_compop-tdprinter = gv_devtype.
      lv_control_parameters-no_dialog = abap_true.
      lv_control_parameters-preview = abap_true.
      lv_control_parameters-getotf = 'X'.

      LOOP AT it_smf INTO wa_smf.
        CALL FUNCTION lv_fnam "'/1BCDWB/SF00000216'
          EXPORTING
            control_parameters = lv_control_parameters
            output_options     = w_compop
            user_settings      = 'X'
            wa_final           = wa_smf
          IMPORTING
            job_output_info    = t_otf_from_fm
          TABLES
            it_final           = it_smf
          EXCEPTIONS
            formatting_error   = 1
            internal_error     = 2
            send_error         = 3
            user_canceled      = 4
            OTHERS             = 5.
        IF sy-subrc <> 0.
          MESSAGE ID sy-msgid TYPE sy-msgty NUMBER sy-msgno
          WITH sy-msgv1 sy-msgv2 sy-msgv3 sy-msgv4.
        ENDIF.
        t_otf[] = t_otf_from_fm-otfdata[].
        CALL FUNCTION 'CONVERT_OTF'
          EXPORTING
            format        = 'PDF'
            max_linewidth = 132
          IMPORTING
            bin_filesize  = w_bin_filesize
          TABLES
            otf           = t_otf
            lines         = t_pdf_tab.
        IF sy-subrc <> 0.
          MESSAGE ID sy-msgid TYPE sy-msgty NUMBER sy-msgno
          WITH sy-msgv1 sy-msgv2 sy-msgv3 sy-msgv4.
        ENDIF.
        SELECT SINGLE name1 FROM lfa1 INTO @DATA(lv_name1) WHERE  lifnr = @wa_smf-lifnr.
*        CONCATENATE p_file  wa_smf-lifnr '-' lv_name1 '.PDF' INTO w_full_path.
        CALL FUNCTION 'GUI_DOWNLOAD'
          EXPORTING
            bin_filesize = w_bin_filesize
            filename     = 'C:\TEMP\Endorse_List.PDF'
*           filename     = w_full_path
            filetype     = 'BIN'
          TABLES
            data_tab     = t_pdf_tab.
        IF sy-subrc <> 0.
          MESSAGE ID sy-msgid TYPE sy-msgty NUMBER sy-msgno
          WITH sy-msgv1 sy-msgv2 sy-msgv3 sy-msgv4.
        ENDIF.
        CLEAR:wa_smf. "changes added by vamshi 29.07.2021
      ENDLOOP.

  ENDCASE.

ENDFORM.
